  in ascertaining whether the judgment granted is more or less
  favourable than the offer to settle, shall not have regard to costs
  awarded in the judgment or that would otherwise be awarded.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application to court

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} For greater certainty, if a written offer to settle
  that does not provide for the settlement of the issue of costs is
  accepted, a party to the offer may apply to the Court for an order
  determining costs.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2005-340, s. 1
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Offer to contribute

**[[421]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-421 .sectionLabel}** Subsection 420(2)
applies to a third party, or to one of two or more defendants who are
alleged to be jointly and severally liable to the plaintiff in respect
of a claim, who makes a written offer to other defendants or third
parties to contribute toward a settlement of the claim.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Disclosure of offer to Court

**[[422]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-422 .sectionLabel}** No communication
respecting an offer to settle or offer to contribute shall be made to
the Court, other than to a case management judge or prothonotary
assigned under rule 383(c) or to a judge or prothonotary at a pre-trial
conference, until all questions of liability and the relief to be
granted, other than costs, have been determined.

## [PART 12]{.HLabel1}[Enforcement of Orders]{.HTitleText1} {#h-1016046 .Part}

### [General]{.HTitleText2} {#h-1016047 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definition of [[designated
officer]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef}

**[[422.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-422.1 .sectionLabel}** In this Part,
[[designated officer]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means an officer of the
Registry designated by an order of the Court.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2021-245, s. 8](#1334006-1336824){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1334006-1336824.html"
  role="button"}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Where brought

**[[423]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-423 .sectionLabel}** All matters relating to
the enforcement of orders shall be brought before the Federal Court.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2004-283, s. 33
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Enforcement of order of tribunal

- **[[424]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-424
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Where under an Act of Parliament the
  Court is authorized to enforce an order of a tribunal and no other
  procedure is required by or under that Act, the order may be enforced
  under this Part.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Filing of order

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} An order referred to in subsection (1) shall be filed
  together with a certificate from the tribunal, or an affidavit of a
  person authorized to file such an order, attesting to the authenticity
  of the order.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Enforcement of order for payment of money

**[[425]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-425 .sectionLabel}** An order for the
payment of money may be enforced by

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} a writ of seizure and sale in Form 425A;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} garnishment proceedings;

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} a charging order;

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} the appointment of a receiver; and

- [(e)]{.lawlabel} in respect of a person referred to in rule 429, a
  writ of sequestration in Form 425B.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Examinations

- **[[426]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-426 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  person who has obtained an order for the payment of money may

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} conduct an oral examination of the judgment debtor
    or, if the judgment debtor is a corporation, of an officer of the
    corporation, as to the judgment debtor's assets; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} bring a motion for leave to conduct an oral
    examination of any other person who might have information regarding
    the judgement debtor's assets.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Service of notice of motion

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} In respect of a motion brought under paragraph
  (1)(b), the notice of motion shall be served on the judgement debtor
  and personally served on the person to be examined.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Criteria for leave

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} On a motion brought under paragraph (1)(b), the Court
  may grant leave to conduct the oral examination and determine the time
  and manner of conducting the examination, if it is satisfied that

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the person to be examined may have information as
    to the judgment debtor's assets;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the moving party has been unable to informally
    obtain the information from the person to be examined or from
    another source by any other reasonable means;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} it would be unfair not to allow the moving party to
    conduct the examination; and

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the examination will not cause undue delay,
    inconvenience or expense to the person to be examined or to the
    judgment debtor.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2021-245, s. 9](#1334008-1336839){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1334008-1336839.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-426-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 426"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Possession of land

- **[[427]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-427 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} An
  order for possession of real property or immoveables may be enforced
  by

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a writ of possession, in Form 427; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in respect of a person referred to in rule 429, an
    order of committal or a writ of sequestration, or both.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Limitation

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A writ of possession shall be issued only if the
  Court is satisfied that every person in possession of the whole or any
  part of the real property or immoveables has received notice
  sufficient to enable the person to apply to the Court for any relief
  to which the person may be entitled.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Delivery of personal property and
movables

- **[[428]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-428 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} An
  order for the delivery of personal property or movables that does not
  give the person against whom the order is made the alternative of
  paying an amount equal to the value of the personal property or
  movables may be enforced by

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a writ of delivery to recover the personal property
    or movables, in Form 428; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in respect of a person referred to in rule 429, an
    order of committal or a writ of sequestration, or both.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Delivery of personal property or
  movables or amount equal to value

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} An order for the delivery of personal property or
  movables or the payment of an amount equal to their value may be
  enforced by

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a writ of delivery to recover the personal property
    or movables or an amount equal to their value, in Form 428; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in respect of a person referred to in rule 429, a
    writ of sequestration.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Writ of sequestration and order of
committal

- **[[429]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-429
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Where a person who is required by an
  order to perform an act within a specified time refuses or neglects to
  do so within that time, or where a person disobeys an order to abstain
  from doing an act, the order may, with the leave of the Court, be
  enforced by

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a writ of sequestration against the property of the
    person;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} where the person is a corporation, a writ of
    sequestration against the property of any director or officer of the
    corporation; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} subject to subsection (2), in respect of an order
    other than for payment of money, an order of committal against the
    person or, if the person is a corporation, against any director or
    officer of the corporation.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Limitation

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Where under an order requiring the delivery of
  personal property or movables a person who is liable to execution has
  the alternative of paying an amount equal to the value of the personal
  property or movables, the order shall not be enforced by an order of
  committal.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2021-245, s. 10(E)](#1334010-1336842){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1334010-1336842.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-429-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 429"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Personal service required

**[[430]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-430 .sectionLabel}** Unless the Court orders
otherwise, an order shall not be enforced against a person under rule
429 unless the order has been personally served on the person.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Performance by other person

**[[431]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-431 .sectionLabel}** Where a person does not
comply with an order to perform an act, without prejudice to the powers
of the Court to punish the person for contempt, on motion, the Court may
order that

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the required act be performed by the person by whom
  the order was obtained or by another person appointed by the Court;
  and

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} the non-complying person pay the costs incurred in
  the performance of the act, ascertained in such a manner as the Court
  may direct, and that a writ of execution be issued against the
  non-complying person for those costs.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Non-performance of condition precedent

**[[432]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-432 .sectionLabel}** Where a person who is
entitled to relief under an order subject to the fulfilment of a
condition fails to fulfil that condition, the person is deemed to have
abandoned the benefit of the order and, unless the Court orders
otherwise, any other interested person may take any step that is
warranted by the order or that might have been taken if the order had
not been made.

### [Writs of Execution]{.HTitleText2} {#h-1016098 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Requisition for writ of execution

- **[[433]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-433
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (2) and rules
  434 and 435, a person entitled to execution may obtain a writ of
  execution by filing a requisition for its issuance.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}When writ may be issued

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A writ of execution shall be issued only if, at the
  time a requisition therefor is filed, any period specified in the
  order for the payment of money or for the doing of an act required
  under the order has expired.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Endorsement on writ

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} A writ of execution for the recovery of money shall
  be endorsed with a direction to the sheriff to levy

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the amount of money due and payable that is sought
    to be recovered;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} any interest thereon that is sought to be
    recovered, from the date of the order; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} any sheriff's fees and costs of execution.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Limitation on issuance

- **[[434]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-434 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  writ of execution to enforce an order shall not be issued without the
  leave of the Court if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} six or more years have elapsed since the date of
    the order;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a change has taken place, by death or otherwise, in
    the persons entitled or liable to execution under the order;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} under the order a person is entitled to relief
    subject to the fulfilment of a condition that is alleged to have
    been fulfilled; or

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} any personal property or movables sought to be
    seized under the writ are in the possession of a receiver appointed
    by the Court or of a sequestrator.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Period of validity of order

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} An order granting leave under subsection (1) expires
  one year after it is made.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Leave to issue writ in aid

**[[435]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-435 .sectionLabel}** A writ of execution in
aid of another writ of execution shall not be issued without the leave
of the Court.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}[Ex parte]{.otherLang lang="la"} motion
for leave to issue writ

**[[436]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-436 .sectionLabel}** A motion for leave to
issue a writ of execution under subsection 434(1) or rule 435 may be
made [ex parte]{.otherLang lang="la"}.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Period of validity of writ

- **[[437]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-437 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  writ of execution is valid for six years after its date of issuance.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Requisition to extend validity of writ

  [(1.1)]{.lawlabel} A person who is entitled to execution of a writ,
  including a writ the validity of which has been previously extended,
  may make a requisition to a designated officer to extend the validity
  of the writ.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Conditions

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The designated officer may extend the validity of a
  writ of execution for a further period of six years if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the requisition is accompanied by an affidavit
    attesting to

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the date on which the writ was issued,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the fact that the writ is not wholly executed,

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} the fact that there has been an attempt to
      execute the writ in the previous six years or that the writ has
      been registered in a provincial registry, and

    - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} if the validity of the writ was previously
      extended, the date on which it was extended;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the writ is not wholly executed; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the writ is not expired.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Extension indicated on writ

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} If the validity of a writ of execution is extended
  under subsection (2), a new copy of the writ, bearing the date on
  which its validity was extended, shall be issued in Form 425A.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Effect of extended writ

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} A writ the validity of which has been extended under
  subsection (2) continues without interruption.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2021-245, s. 11](#1334012-1336857){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1334012-1336857.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-437-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 437"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Advance or security required

**[[438]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-438 .sectionLabel}** Before executing a writ
of execution, a sheriff to whom the writ is directed may require the
person at whose instance it was issued to make an advance, or to give
security, sufficient to cover the costs of execution.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notice to sheriff

- **[[439]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-439 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  person at whose instance a writ of execution is issued may serve a
  notice on the sheriff to whom the writ is directed requiring the
  sheriff, within such time as may be specified in the notice, to
  endorse on the writ a statement of the manner in which the sheriff has
  executed it and to send a copy of the statement to the person.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Order to sheriff to comply

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Where a sheriff fails to comply with a notice served
  under subsection (1), the person by whom it was served may apply to
  the Court for an order directing the sheriff to comply with the
  notice.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Directions from Court

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} A person at whose instance a writ of execution is
  issued, a sheriff or any interested person may seek directions from
  the Court concerning any issue that is not addressed by these Rules
  that arises from the enforcement of an order.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2013-18, s. 15
- [SOR/2021-245, s. 12](#1334014-1336860){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1334014-1336860.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-439-20130208.html "Link to previous version of section 439"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Multiple writs for single order

**[[440]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-440 .sectionLabel}** Writs of execution of
different types may be issued to enforce a single order, where the terms
of the order so require.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Leave to issue writ of sequestration

- **[[441]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-441 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} No
  writ of sequestration shall be issued without leave of a judge.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Personal service of notice

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Notice of a motion for leave to issue a writ of
  sequestration shall be personally served on the person against whose
  property it is sought to issue the writ.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Multiple writs of seizure and sale

- **[[442]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-442 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  person who is entitled to enforce an order by a writ of seizure and
  sale may request the issuance of two or more such writs directed to
  the sheriffs of different geographical areas, either at the same time
  or at different times, to enforce the order, but no greater total
  amount shall be levied under all such writs than would be authorized
  to be levied under a single writ.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Different geographical areas

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Where a person requests the issuance of two or more
  writs of seizure and sale directed to sheriffs of different
  geographical areas to enforce the same order, the person shall inform
  each sheriff of the issuance of the other writ or writs.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Second writ where sum unascertained

**[[443]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-443 .sectionLabel}** Where the payment of an
ascertained sum of money and an unascertained sum of money or costs is
ordered, if, at the time when the ascertained sum becomes payable, the
unascertained sum or costs have not been assessed, the person who is
entitled to enforce the order may request the issuance of a writ of
seizure and sale to enforce payment of the ascertained sum and, after
the unascertained sum or costs have been assessed, may request the
issuance of a second writ to enforce payment thereof.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Order under \$200

**[[444]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-444 .sectionLabel}** Where an order for
payment of less than \$200 does not entitle the plaintiff to costs
against the person against whom a writ of seizure and sale to enforce
the order is issued, the writ may not authorize the sheriff to whom it
is directed to levy any fees or costs of execution.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Sale of interest in property

**[[445]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-445 .sectionLabel}** Any interest of a
judgment debtor in property may be sold under a writ of seizure and
sale.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Sale of real property or immoveables

**[[446]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-446 .sectionLabel}** Real property or
immoveables shall not be sold under a writ of seizure and sale within a
shorter period than that provided for by the laws of the province in
which the real property or immoveables are situated or any longer period
ordered by the Court.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Property bound by writ

**[[447]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-447 .sectionLabel}** Property is bound for
the purpose of execution of an order as of the date of the delivery to
the sheriff of a writ of seizure and sale.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Laws of province apply

**[[448]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-448 .sectionLabel}** In seizing, advertising
for sale or selling property, a sheriff shall, except as otherwise
provided in the writ or in these Rules, follow the laws applicable to
the execution of similar writs issued by a superior court of the
province in which the property was seized.

### [Garnishment Proceedings]{.HTitleText2} {#h-1016168 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notice of garnishment

- **[[449]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-449
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to rules 452 and 456 and on
  requisition filed by a judgment creditor in Form 449A, a designated
  officer may issue a notice of garnishment, in Form 449B, for the
  attachment of the following debts to satisfy an order for the payment
  of money:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a debt owing or accruing from a person in Canada to
    a judgment debtor; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a debt owing or accruing from a person outside
    Canada to a judgment debtor, if the debt is one for which the person
    might be sued in Canada by the judgment debtor.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Requisition --- notice of garnishment

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The requisition shall be accompanied by a copy of the
  order for the payment of money and an affidavit that contains the
  following information:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the date and amount of any payment received since
    the order was made;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the amount owing, including postjudgment interest;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the manner in which the amount owing and the
    postjudgment interest are calculated;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the address of the judgment debtor;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} the name and address of each garnishee;

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} a statement indicating that the judgment creditor
    believes that the garnishees are or will become indebted to the
    judgment debtor and the grounds for the belief;

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} if a garnishee is not then indebted but will become
    indebted to the judgment debtor, details with respect to the date on
    and the circumstances under which the debt will arise;

  - [(h)]{.lawlabel} details of the debts; and

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} any other information that is necessary to
    establish the amount awarded and the judgment creditor's
    entitlement.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Service

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The judgment creditor shall serve on each garnishee
  and on the judgment debtor a copy of the notice of garnishment and a
  copy of the requisition.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Debts bound as of time of service

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} Subject to rule 452, a notice of garnishment binds
  the debts attached as of the time of its service on the garnishee.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}No payment to judgment debtor

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} Subject to rule 452, a garnishee who has been served
  with a notice of garnishment shall not pay the judgment debtor any
  amount owing to the judgment debtor without leave of the Court.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Sworn declaration of garnishee

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} Within 21 days after the day on which the garnishee
  is served with the notice of garnishment, the garnishee shall file and
  serve on the judgment creditor and judgment debtor a sworn declaration
  of garnishee, in Form 449C, that contains

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a list of all debts owing or accruing to the
    judgment debtor by the garnishee by reason of an obligation incurred
    on or before the day of the garnishee's declaration; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} if the garnishee disputes liability to pay a debt
    claimed to be owing or accruing to the judgment debtor or claims the
    debt is for a lesser amount than that set out in the notice of
    garnishment, any relevant information, including any supporting
    documents not contained in the requisition for the issuance of a
    notice of garnishment.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2021-245, s. 13](#1334016-1336888){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1334016-1336888.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-449-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 449"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Order or certificate not to be contested

**[[449.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-449.1 .sectionLabel}** In a proceeding
under any of rules 449 to 465, a judgment debtor shall not contest the
order or certificate that gave rise to the garnishment.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2021-245, s. 13](#1334016-1336892){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1334016-1336892.html"
  role="button"}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Payment into Court by garnishee

**[[450]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-450 .sectionLabel}** A garnishee who admits
liability for a debt due to a judgment debtor shall pay into court the
debt, or as much of the debt as is sufficient to satisfy the judgment,
and give notice of the payment to the judgment creditor.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2021-245, s. 13](#1334016-1336896){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1334016-1336896.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-450-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 450"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Garnishment order

- **[[451]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-451 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If a
  garnishee does not file a declaration of garnishee under subsection
  449(6) or make the payment into court under rule 450, the Court may,
  on motion by the judgment creditor, order the garnishee to pay the
  amount owing to the judgment creditor as if the garnishee were the
  judgment debtor.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Future payment

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} If a debt owed to a judgment debtor is payable at a
  future time or is subject to a condition when a notice of garnishment
  is issued, an order under subsection (1) may require that the
  garnishee pay the debt to the judgment creditor when the debt becomes
  payable or the condition is fulfilled.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Enforcement

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} An order under subsection (1) may be enforced in the
  same manner as any other order for the payment of money.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2021-245, s. 13](#1334016-1336905){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1334016-1336905.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-451-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 451"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exemption from seizure

**[[452]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-452 .sectionLabel}** If a debt owing or
accruing to a judgment debtor is in respect of wages or salary, no
portion of the wages or salary that is exempt from seizure or attachment
under the law of the province where the debt is payable shall be
attached under a notice of garnishment.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2021-245, s. 13](#1334016-1336909){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1334016-1336909.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-452-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 452"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Summary determination of liability

- **[[453]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-453 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If a
  garnishee disputes liability to pay a debt claimed to be owing or
  accruing to the judgment debtor or claims that the debt is for a
  lesser amount than that set out in the notice of garnishment, the
  Court may, on motion, summarily determine the garnishee's liability or
  order that it be determined in any manner that the Court directs.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Service and filing

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The party bringing the motion shall serve and file
  the notice of motion on all other parties within the following time
  limit:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a motion brought by the judgment
    creditor or judgment debtor, within 21 days after the day on which
    that party is served with the declaration of garnishee; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a motion brought by the garnishee,
    within 21 days after the day on which the judgment creditor or
    judgment debtor is served with the declaration of garnishee,
    whichever is the earlier.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2021-245, s. 13](#1334016-1336918){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1334016-1336918.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-453-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 453"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Discharge of liability

**[[454]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-454 .sectionLabel}** A payment made by a
garnishee under rule 450 or in compliance with a notice of garnishment
issued under subsection 449(1) or an order under rule 451 or 453, and
any execution levied against a garnishee under such a notice of
garnishment or order, constitutes a valid discharge of the garnishee's
liability to the judgment debtor to the extent of the amount paid or
levied, even if the attachment is later set aside or the order or notice
of garnishment from which it arose is later reversed.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2021-245, s. 13](#1334016-1336922){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1334016-1336922.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-454-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 454"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Other interested person

- **[[455]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-455 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  person, other than a judgment debtor, who claims to have an interest
  in the debt sought to be attached may, by motion, state the nature of
  their interest and the relief sought.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Determination of validity of claim

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} On motion brought under subsection (1), the Court may
  summarily determine the questions at issue between the claimants or
  order that they be determined in any manner that it directs.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2005-340, s. 2
- [SOR/2021-245, s. 13](#1334016-1336928){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1334016-1336928.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-455-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 455"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Order for payment

- **[[456]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-456 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  judgment creditor shall not file a requisition under subsection 449(1)
  in respect of money that is standing to the credit of the judgment
  debtor in court. However, they may bring a motion for an order that
  the money, or a lesser amount sufficient to satisfy the order sought
  to be enforced and the costs of the motion, be paid to the judgment
  creditor.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Limitation

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} If a motion is brought under subsection (1), the
  money to which the motion relates shall not be paid out of court until
  after the determination of the motion.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Service of notice of motion

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Unless the Court directs otherwise, the notice of a
  motion brought under subsection (1) shall be served on the judgment
  debtor and filed at least seven days before the day fixed for the
  hearing of the motion.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2021-245, s. 13](#1334016-1336937){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1334016-1336937.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-456-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 456"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Costs related to requisition

**[[457]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-457 .sectionLabel}** Unless the Court
directs otherwise, the costs of a requisition under subsection 449(1)
and any related proceedings shall be retained by the judgment creditor
out of the money recovered and be in priority to the judgment debt.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2021-245, s. 13](#1334016-1336941){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1334016-1336941.html"
  role="button"}
:::

Version](section-457-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 457"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Charging Orders]{.HTitleText2} {#h-1016214 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Order for interim charge and show cause

- **[[458]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-458 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} On
  the [ex parte]{.otherLang lang="la"} motion of a judgment creditor,
  the Court may, for the purpose of enforcing an order for the payment
  of an ascertained sum of money,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} make an order imposing an interim charge for
    securing payment of that sum and any interest thereon

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} on real property or immoveables, or on an
      interest in real property or a right in immoveables, if the
      judgment debtor, directly or indirectly, owns the real property or
      immoveables, holds an interest in the real property, including a
      beneficial interest, or holds an immoveable right or has a claim
      to the immoveables, including as beneficiary under a trust or
      succession, in Form 458A, or

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} on any interest or right, including a beneficial
      interest, in any shares, bonds or other securities specified in
      the order, to which the judgment debtor is directly or indirectly
      entitled, in Form 458B; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} order the judgment debtor to show cause, at a
    specified time and place, why the charge should not be made
    absolute.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Service of show cause order

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Unless the Court directs otherwise, an order made
  under subsection (1) shall be served on the judgment debtor and, where
  the order relates to property referred to in subparagraph (1)(a)(ii),
  on the corporation, government or other person or entity by whom the
  securities were issued, at least seven days before the time appointed
  for the hearing.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2021-245, s. 14](#1334018-1336945){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1334018-1336945.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-458-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 458"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Show cause hearing

- **[[459]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-459 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} At a
  show cause hearing referred to in paragraph 458(1)(b), the Court shall
  make the interim charge absolute, in Form 459, or discharge it.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Enforcement of charging order

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A charge made absolute has the same effect, and is
  enforceable in the same manner, as a charge made by the judgment
  debtor.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Disposition by judgment debtor

**[[460]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-460 .sectionLabel}** No disposition by a
judgment debtor of an interest in property subject to an interim or
absolute charge under rule 458 or 459 is valid against the judgment
creditor.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Transfer of securities prohibited

- **[[461]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-461
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Unless the Court orders otherwise,
  no person or entity on whom an order was served under subsection
  458(2) shall permit the transfer of any security specified in the
  order or pay to any person a dividend or any interest payable thereon.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Liability of transferor

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} If, after service of an order under rule 458, a
  person or entity on whom it was served makes a transfer or payment
  prohibited by subsection (1), the person or entity shall be liable to
  pay to the judgment creditor an amount equal to the value of the
  security transferred or the amount of the payment made, as the case
  may be, or as much of it as is sufficient to satisfy the judgment
  debt.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Discharge or variance of charging order

**[[462]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-462 .sectionLabel}** The Court may, on the
motion of a judgment debtor or any other person with an interest in
property subject to an interim or absolute charge under rule 458 or 459,
at any time, discharge or vary the charging order on such terms as to
costs as it considers just.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Charge on interest in money paid into
court

- **[[463]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-463 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} On
  motion, the Court may, for the purpose of enforcing an order for the
  payment of an ascertained sum of money, by order, impose a charge for
  securing payment of the amount due under the order, and of any
  interest thereon, on any interest to which the judgment debtor is
  beneficially entitled in any money paid into court that is identified
  in the order.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application of rules re other charging
  orders

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subsection 458(1) and rules 460 and 462 apply, with
  such modifications as are necessary, to an order made under this rule.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Ancillary or incidental injunction

**[[464]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-464 .sectionLabel}** On motion, a judge may
grant an injunction ancillary or incidental to a charging order under
rule 458 or appoint a receiver to enforce a charge imposed by such an
order.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Order prohibiting dealing with funds

- **[[465]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-465 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  Court, on the motion of a person

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} who has a mortgage or charge on the interest of
    another person in money paid into court,

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} to whom such an interest has been assigned, or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} who is a judgment creditor of a person entitled to
    such an interest,

  may make an order prohibiting the transfer, delivery, payment or other
  dealing with all or any part of the money, or any income thereon,
  without prior notice to the moving party.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Service of notice of motion

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Notice of a motion under subsection (1) shall be
  served on every person whose interest may be affected by the order
  sought.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Costs

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} On a motion under subsection (1), the Court may order
  the moving party to pay the costs of any party or of any other person
  interested in the money in question.

### [Contempt Orders]{.HTitleText2} {#h-1016256 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Contempt

**[[466]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-466 .sectionLabel}** Subject to rule 467, a
person is guilty of contempt of Court who

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} at a hearing fails to maintain a respectful attitude,
  remain silent or refrain from showing approval or disapproval of the
  proceeding;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} disobeys a process or order of the Court;

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} acts in such a way as to interfere with the orderly
  administration of justice, or to impair the authority or dignity of
  the Court;

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} is an officer of the Court and fails to perform his
  or her duty; or

- [(e)]{.lawlabel} is a sheriff or bailiff and does not execute a writ
  forthwith or does not make a return thereof or, in executing it,
  infringes a rule the contravention of which renders the sheriff or
  bailiff liable to a penalty.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Right to a hearing

- **[[467]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-467
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to rule 468, before a person
  may be found in contempt of Court, the person alleged to be in
  contempt shall be served with an order, made on the motion of a person
  who has an interest in the proceeding or at the Court's own
  initiative, requiring the person alleged to be in contempt

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} to appear before a judge at a time and place
    stipulated in the order;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} to be prepared to hear proof of the act with which
    the person is charged, which shall be described in the order with
    sufficient particularity to enable the person to know the nature of
    the case against the person; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} to be prepared to present any defence that the
    person may have.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}[Ex parte]{.otherLang lang="la"} motion

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A motion for an order under subsection (1) may be
  made [ex parte]{.otherLang lang="la"}.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Burden of proof

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} An order may be made under subsection (1) if the
  Court is satisfied that there is a [prima facie]{.otherLang lang="la"}
  case that contempt has been committed.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Service of contempt order

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} An order under subsection (1) shall be personally
  served, together with any supporting documents, unless otherwise
  ordered by the Court.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Contempt in presence of a judge

**[[468]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-468 .sectionLabel}** In a case of urgency, a
person may be found in contempt of Court for an act committed in the
presence of a judge and condemned at once, if the person has been called
on to justify his or her behaviour.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Burden of proof

**[[469]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-469 .sectionLabel}** A finding of contempt
shall be based on proof beyond a reasonable doubt.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Evidence to be oral

- **[[470]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-470
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Unless the Court directs otherwise,
  evidence on a motion for a contempt order, other than an order under
  subsection 467(1), shall be oral.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Testimony not compellable

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A person alleged to be in contempt may not be
  compelled to testify.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Assistance of Attorney General

**[[471]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-471 .sectionLabel}** Where the Court
considers it necessary, it may request the assistance of the Attorney
General of Canada in relation to any proceedings for contempt.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Penalty

**[[472]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-472 .sectionLabel}** Where a person is found
to be in contempt, a judge may order that

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the person be imprisoned for a period of less than
  five years or until the person complies with the order;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} the person be imprisoned for a period of less than
  five years if the person fails to comply with the order;

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} the person pay a fine;

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} the person do or refrain from doing any act;

- [(e)]{.lawlabel} in respect of a person referred to in rule 429, the
  person's property be sequestered; and

- [(f)]{.lawlabel} the person pay costs.

### [Process of the Court]{.HTitleText2} {#h-1016295 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}To whom process may be issued

- **[[473]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-473
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Where there is no sheriff or a
  sheriff is unable or unwilling to act, a process, including a warrant
  for arrest of property under rule 481, may be issued to any person to
  whom a process of a superior court of the province in which the
  process is to be executed could be issued.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Execution of process

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Where a process is issued to a sheriff, it may, at
  the sheriff's direction, be executed by a person authorized under
  provincial law to execute the process of a superior court of the
  province in which the process is to be executed.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Certificate of judgment

- **[[474]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-474
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Where an order made against the
  Crown for the payment of money for costs or otherwise is executory and

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} where no appeal of the order has been instituted,
    the time allowed by law for an appeal from the order has expired, or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} where there has been an appeal from the order, the
    order has been affirmed or varied on appeal,

  the Administrator shall issue a certificate of judgment accordingly.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Delivery of certificate

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A certificate issued under subsection (1) shall be
  transmitted by the Administrator to the office of the Deputy Attorney
  General of Canada.

## [PART 13]{.HLabel1}[Admiralty Actions]{.HTitleText1} {#h-1016309 .Part}

### [Application of this Part]{.HTitleText2} {#h-1016310 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application

- **[[475]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-475 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} This
  Part applies to Admiralty actions.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application of other rules

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Except to the extent that they are inconsistent with
  this Part, the rules applicable to other actions apply to Admiralty
  actions.

### [Definitions]{.HTitleText2} {#h-1016316 .Subheading}

Definition of [[designated officer]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

**[[476]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-476 .sectionLabel}** In this Part,
[[designated officer]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means an officer of the
Registry designated by an order of the Court.

### [Actions [In Rem]{.otherLang lang="la"} and [In Personam]{.otherLang lang="la"}]{.HTitleText2} {#h-1016319 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Types of admiralty actions

- **[[477]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-477
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Admiralty actions may be [in
  rem]{.otherLang lang="la"} or [in personam]{.otherLang lang="la"}, or
  both.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Style of cause of action [in
  rem]{.otherLang lang="la"}

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The style of cause of an action [in rem]{.otherLang
  lang="la"} shall be in Form 477.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Style of cause of action [in
  personam]{.otherLang lang="la"}

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The style of cause of an action [in
  personam]{.otherLang lang="la"} shall be as provided for in subsection
  67(2).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Defendants in action in
  [rem]{.otherLang lang="la"}

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} In an action in [rem]{.otherLang lang="la"}, a
  plaintiff shall include as a defendant the owners and all others
  interested in the subject-matter of the action.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Action against more than one ship

**[[478]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-478 .sectionLabel}** In an action against
more than one ship in accordance with subsection 43(8) of the Act, each
ship shall be named as a defendant in the statement of claim.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Service of statement of claim

- **[[479]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-479
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (2), the
  statement of claim in an action [in rem]{.otherLang lang="la"} shall
  be served

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in respect of a ship or cargo or other property on
    board a ship, by attaching a certified copy of the statement of
    claim to some conspicuous part of the ship;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in respect of cargo or other property that is not
    on board a ship, by attaching a certified copy of the statement of
    claim to the cargo or property;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} in respect of freight,

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} if the cargo in respect of which the freight is
      owing is on board a ship, by attaching a certified copy of the
      statement of claim to a conspicuous part of the ship,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} if the cargo in respect of which the freight is
      owing is not on board a ship, by attaching a certified copy of the
      statement of claim to the cargo, or

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} if monies payable for the freight are in the
      possession of a person, by personal service of the statement of
      claim on that person; and

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} in respect of any proceeds paid into court in
    another proceeding, by filing a certified copy of the statement of
    claim in that proceeding.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Alternate service of statement of claim

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} If access cannot be obtained to property in respect
  of which a statement of claim is to be served under subsection (1),
  the statement of claim may be served personally on a person who
  appears to be in charge of the property.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Defence of action [in rem]{.otherLang
lang="la"}

- **[[480]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-480 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} An
  action [in rem]{.otherLang lang="la"} against a ship or other thing
  named as a defendant in the action may be defended only by a person
  who claims to be the owner of the ship or thing or to be otherwise
  interested therein.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Interest to be pleaded

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A defence filed by a person referred to in
  subsection (1) shall disclose the interest that the person claims in
  the ship or thing.

### [Arrest of Property]{.HTitleText2} {#h-1016348 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Warrant for the arrest of property

- **[[481]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-481 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  designated officer may issue a warrant for the arrest of property in
  an action [in rem]{.otherLang lang="la"}, in Form 481, at any time
  after the filing of a statement of claim.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Affidavit

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A party seeking a warrant under subsection (1) shall
  file an affidavit, entitled "Affidavit to Lead Warrant", stating

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the name, address and occupation of the party;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the nature of the claim and the basis for invoking
    the [in rem]{.otherLang lang="la"} jurisdiction of the Court;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} that the claim has not been satisfied;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the nature of the property to be arrested and,
    where the property is a ship, the name and nationality of the ship
    and the port to which it belongs; and

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} where, pursuant to subsection 43(8) of the Act, the
    warrant is sought against a ship that is not the subject of the
    action, that the deponent has reasonable grounds to believe that the
    ship against which the warrant is sought is beneficially owned by
    the person who is the owner of the ship that is the subject of the
    action.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Service

- **[[482]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-482 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  warrant issued under subsection 481(1), the Affidavit to Lead Warrant
  and the statement of claim in the action shall be served together by a
  sheriff in the manner set out in rule 479, whereupon the property
  subject to the warrant is deemed to be arrested.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Proof of service

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Proof of service of the documents referred to in
  subsection (1) shall be filed forthwith after the documents are
  served.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Possession and responsibility

- **[[483]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-483
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (2),
  possession of, and responsibility for, property arrested under
  subsection 482(1) does not vest in the sheriff but continues in the
  person in possession of the property immediately before the arrest.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Order for possession of arrested
  property

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The Court may order a sheriff to take possession of
  arrested property on condition that a party assume responsibility for
  any costs or fees incurred or payable in carrying out the order and
  give security satisfactory to the Court for the payment thereof.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Prohibition against moving arrested
property

**[[484]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-484 .sectionLabel}** No property arrested
under a warrant shall be moved without leave of the Court or the consent
of all parties and caveators.

### [Bail]{.HTitleText2} {#h-1016371 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Release of arrested property

**[[485]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-485 .sectionLabel}** On motion, the Court
may fix the amount of bail to be given for the release of arrested
property.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Form of bail

- **[[486]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-486
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Unless the parties agree otherwise,
  bail shall consist of

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the guaranty of a bank;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the bond of a surety company licensed to do
    business in Canada or to furnish security bonds in the part of
    Canada where the bond is executed, in Form 486A; or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} a bail bond in Form 486A.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notice of bail

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A party who intends to give bail in the form of a
  bond referred to in paragraph (1)(b) or (c) shall serve and file a
  notice of bail, in Form 486B, at least 24 hours before filing the
  bond.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notice of objection to bail

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} An adverse party or caveator who is not satisfied
  with the sufficiency of a bond set out in a notice of bail shall serve
  and file a notice of objection in Form 486C.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Sufficiency of bail bond

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} Any question as to the form of bail or the
  sufficiency of a surety may be determined by a designated officer or
  referred by that officer to the Court.

### [Release from Arrest]{.HTitleText2} {#h-1016386 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Release of arrested property

- **[[487]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-487
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Unless a caveat has been filed under
  subsection 493(2), a designated officer may issue a release of
  arrested property in Form 487

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} on payment into court of

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the amount claimed,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the appraised value of the property arrested, or

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} where cargo is arrested for freight only, the
      amount of the freight, verified by affidavit;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} if bail has been given in an amount fixed under
    rule 485 and in accordance with subsections 486(1) and (2) and no
    objection under subsection 486(3) is outstanding;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} on the consent in writing of the party at whose
    instance the property was arrested; or

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} on the discontinuance or dismissal of the action in
    respect of which the property was arrested.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Referral to judge or prothonotary

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Where a release is sought under subsection (1), a
  designated officer may refer the matter to a judge or prothonotary.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Release at any time

- **[[488]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-488 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} On
  motion, the Court may, at any time, order the release of arrested
  property.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Release of ship

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Where, pursuant to subsection 43(8) of the Act, a
  ship that is not the subject of an action has been arrested, any owner
  or other person interested in the ship may bring a motion to the Court
  for the release of the ship, and if it is found that the ship is not
  beneficially owned by the person who is the owner of the ship that is
  the subject of the action, the Court shall order its release without
  the taking of bail.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Release of ship without bail

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Where on a motion under subsection (2) the Court is
  satisfied that the action in which the ship has been arrested is for a
  claim referred to in any of paragraphs 22(2)(a) to (c) of the Act, the
  Court may order the release of the ship without the taking of bail.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Release from arrest

**[[489]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-489 .sectionLabel}** Property shall be
released from arrest on service of a release on the sheriff and payment
of all fees and costs of the sheriff in respect of the arrest or custody
of the property.

### [Sale of Arrested Property]{.HTitleText2} {#h-1016408 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Disposition of arrested property

- **[[490]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-490 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} On
  motion, the Court may order, in respect of property under arrest, that

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the property be appraised and sold, or sold without
    appraisal, by public auction or private contract;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the property be advertised for sale in accordance
    with such directions as may be set out in the order, which may
    include a direction that

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} offers to purchase be under seal and addressed to
      the sheriff,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} offers to purchase all be opened at the same
      time in open court, that the parties be notified of that time and
      that the sale be made pursuant to an order of the Court made at
      that time or after the parties have had an opportunity to be
      heard,

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} the sale not necessarily be to the highest or
      any other bidder, or

    - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} after the opening of the offers and after
      hearing from the parties, if it is doubtful that a fair price has
      been offered, the amount of the highest offer be communicated to
      the other persons who made offers or to some other class of
      persons or that other steps be taken to obtain a higher offer;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the property be sold without advertisement;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} an agent be employed to sell the property, subject
    to such conditions as are stipulated in the order or subject to
    subsequent approval by the Court, on such terms as to compensation
    of the agent as may be stipulated in the order;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} any steps be taken for the safety and preservation
    of the property;

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} where the property is deteriorating in value, it be
    sold forthwith;

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} where the property is on board a ship, it be
    removed or discharged;

  - [(h)]{.lawlabel} where the property is perishable, it be disposed of
    on such terms as the Court may order; or

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the property be inspected in accordance with rule
    249.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Commission

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The appraisal or sale of property under arrest shall
  be effected under the authority of a commission addressed to the
  sheriff in Form 490.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Sale free from liens

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Property sold under subsection (1) is free of any
  liens under Canadian maritime law.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Execution of commission

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} As soon as possible after the execution of a
  commission referred to in subsection (2), the sheriff shall

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} file the commission with a return setting out the
    manner in which it was executed;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} pay into court the proceeds of the sale; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} file the sheriff's accounts and vouchers in support
    thereof.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Sheriff's accounts

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} An assessment officer shall assess the sheriff's
  accounts and report the amount that the assessment officer considers
  should be allowed.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Assessment

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} Any party or caveator who is interested in the
  proceeds of sale referred to in subsection (4) may be heard on an
  assessment under subsection (5).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Review of assessment

  [(7)]{.lawlabel} On motion, the Court may review an assessment done
  under subsection (5).

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Payment out of money paid into court

**[[491]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-491 .sectionLabel}** On a motion for payment
out of any money paid into court under subsection 490(4), the Court may

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} determine the rights of all claimants thereto;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} order payment of all or part of the money to any
  claimant; and

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} order immediate payment of any fees or costs of the
  sheriff in connection with the arrest, custody, appraisal or sale of
  property, including expenses incurred in maintaining the property
  between the time of arrest and the sale of the property.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Directions

- **[[492]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-492 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  Court may, in making an order under rule 490 or 491 or at any time
  thereafter, give directions as to

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} notice to be given to possible claimants to the
    proceeds of sale;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} advertising for other such claimants;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the time within which claimants must file their
    claims; and

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the procedure to be followed in determining the
    rights of the parties.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Claims barred

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A claim that is not made within the time limited and
  in the manner prescribed by an order of the Court under subsection (1)
  is barred, and the Court may proceed to determine other claims and
  distribute the money among the parties entitled thereto without
  reference to any claim so barred.

### [Caveats]{.HTitleText2} {#h-1016454 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Caveat warrant

- **[[493]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-493 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  person who desires to prevent the arrest of property shall serve and
  file a caveat warrant in Form 493A undertaking to give, within three
  days after being required to do so, bail in respect of any action that
  has been, or may be, brought against the property.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Caveat release

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A person who desires to prevent the release of any
  property under arrest shall serve and file a caveat release in Form
  493B.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Caveat payment

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} A person who desires to prevent the payment of money
  out of court shall serve and file a caveat payment in Form 493C.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Service of caveat

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} A caveat under subsection (1), (2) or (3) shall be
  served on all parties and caveators.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Caveat by non-party

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} Where a person filing a caveat under this rule is not
  a party to the action, the caveat shall state the person's name and
  provide an address for service.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Liability of person requesting warrant

- **[[494]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-494 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  person at whose instance a warrant is issued for the arrest of
  property in respect of which there is a caveat warrant outstanding is
  liable to payment of all resulting costs and damages, unless the
  person can satisfy the Court that the person should not be liable
  therefor.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Liability of party requesting caveat

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A person who files a caveat release or caveat payment
  is liable to payment of all resulting costs and damages, unless the
  person can satisfy the Court that the person should not be liable
  therefor.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Expiration of caveat

- **[[495]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-495 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  caveat expires one year after the day on which it was filed.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Filing of new caveat

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A new caveat may be served and filed before or after
  the expiration of an existing caveat.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Withdrawal of caveat

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} A person who has filed a caveat may withdraw it at
  any time by filing a notice in Form 495.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Setting aside of caveat

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} On motion, the Court may order that a caveat be set
  aside.

### [Limitation of Liability]{.HTitleText2} {#h-1016480 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application under s 33(1) of the [Marine
Liability Act](/eng/acts/M-0.7)

- **[[496]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-496 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  party bringing an application under subsection 33(1) of the [Marine
  Liability Act](/eng/acts/M-0.7) shall bring it as an action against
  those claimants whose identity is known to the party.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Motion for directions re service

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A party referred to in subsection (1) may bring an
  [ex parte]{.otherLang lang="la"} motion for directions respecting
  service on possible claimants where the number of possible claimants
  is large or the identity of all possible claimants is unknown to the
  party.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2004-283, s. 37
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Motion to vary or add

**[[497]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-497 .sectionLabel}** A claimant who did not
have notice of an action under subsection 496(1) may, within 10 days
after obtaining notice of an order made under subsection 496(2), serve
and file a notice of motion requesting to be added as a party to the
action.

### [Actions for Collision]{.HTitleText2} {#h-1016490 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Action for collision between ships

- **[[498]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-498
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Unless otherwise ordered by the
  Court, in an action in respect of a collision between ships,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the statement of claim need not contain any more
    particulars concerning the collision than are necessary to identify
    it to the other parties;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the statement of defence need not contain any
    particulars concerning the collision;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} a preliminary act shall accompany a statement of
    claim and a statement of defence or be filed within 10 days after
    the filing of the statement of claim or the statement of defence, as
    the case may be; and

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} a preliminary act shall be contained in a sealed
    envelope bearing the style of cause.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Preliminary act

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A preliminary act shall state

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the date of the collision;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the time of the collision at the location of the
    collision;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the location of the collision;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the names of the ships that collided;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} in respect of the ship of the party filing the
    preliminary act,

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the name of the ship,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the port of registry of the ship,

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} the name of the master of the ship at time of
      the collision,

    - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} the name and address of the person who was in
      command at the time of the collision and during the period
      immediately before the collision,

    - [(v)]{.lawlabel} the names and addresses of any persons keeping
      lookout at the time of the collision and during the period
      immediately before the collision,

    - [(vi)]{.lawlabel} the course of the ship or, if the ship was
      stationary, its heading, at the time when the other ship was first
      seen or immediately before any measures were taken with reference
      to the other ship's presence, whichever was the earlier,

    - [(vii)]{.lawlabel} the speed of the ship through the water at the
      time when the other ship was first seen or immediately before any
      measures were taken with reference to the other ship's presence,
      whichever was the earlier,

    - [(viii)]{.lawlabel} any alteration made to course after the time
      referred to in subparagraph (vi) or during the period immediately
      before the collision, and the time at which the alteration was
      made,

    - [(ix)]{.lawlabel} any alteration made to the speed of the ship
      after the time referred to in subparagraph (vii) or during the
      period immediately before the collision, and the time at which the
      alteration was made,

    - [(x)]{.lawlabel} any measure taken to avoid the collision, and the
      time at which the measure was taken,

    - [(xi)]{.lawlabel} any sound or visual signals given, and the time
      at which the signals were given, and

    - [(xii)]{.lawlabel} the lights carried by the ship and the lights
      it was showing at the time of the collision and during the period
      immediately before the collision;

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} in respect of every other ship involved in the
    collision,

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the name of the ship,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the ship's distance and bearing at the time when
      its echo was first observed by radar by a person on the ship of
      the party filing the preliminary act,

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} the ship's distance, bearing and approximate
      heading when it was first seen by a person on the ship of the
      party filing the preliminary act,

    - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} the lights the ship was showing when it was
      first seen by a person on the ship of the party filing the
      preliminary act,

    - [(v)]{.lawlabel} the lights the ship was showing from the time
      referred to in subparagraph (iv) to the time of the collision,

    - [(vi)]{.lawlabel} any alteration made to the ship's course after
      it was first seen by a person on the ship of the party filing the
      preliminary act, and the time at which the alteration was made,

    - [(vii)]{.lawlabel} any alteration made to the ship's speed after
      it was first seen by a person on the ship of the party filing the
      preliminary act, and the time at which the alteration was made,

    - [(viii)]{.lawlabel} any measure that the ship took to avoid the
      collision, and the time at which the measure was taken,

    - [(ix)]{.lawlabel} any sound or visual signals given, and the time
      at which the signals were given, and

    - [(x)]{.lawlabel} any fault or default attributed to the ship;

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} the state of the weather at the time of the
    collision and during the period immediately before the collision;

  - [(h)]{.lawlabel} the extent of visibility at the time of the
    collision and during the period immediately before the collision;

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the state, direction and force of the tide, or of
    the current if the collision occurred in non-tidal waters, at the
    time of the collision and during the period immediately before the
    collision;

  - [(j)]{.lawlabel} the direction and force of the wind at the time of
    the collision and during the period immediately before the
    collision;

  - [(k)]{.lawlabel} the parts of each ship that first came into
    contact; and

  - [(l)]{.lawlabel} the approximate angle, as illustrated by an
    appropriate sketch annexed to the preliminary act, between the ships
    at the moment of contact.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Form of preliminary act

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The contents of a preliminary act shall be set out in
  parallel columns and, wherever possible, stated in numerical values.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Opening of envelopes containing
  preliminary acts

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} The Administrator shall open the envelopes containing
  the preliminary acts after the pleadings have been closed and all
  preliminary acts have been filed or, with the consent of all parties,
  at any other time.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Order to open envelopes containing
  preliminary acts

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} The Court may, on motion brought after all
  preliminary acts have been filed but before pleadings have been
  closed, order that the Administrator open the envelopes containing the
  preliminary acts.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Endorsement of preliminary act

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} On the opening of an envelope containing a
  preliminary act, the Administrator shall endorse the preliminary act
  with the date on which it was filed, the date on which the envelope
  was opened and the date on which any order was made, or consent filed,
  pursuant to which the envelope was opened.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Deemed part of statement of claim or
  defence

  [(7)]{.lawlabel} A preliminary act shall be read with and form a part
  of the statement of claim or statement of defence, as the case may be,
  as though it were a schedule thereto.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Security not required

**[[499]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-499 .sectionLabel}** Notwithstanding rule
416, a seaman suing for wages or for the loss of clothing and effects in
a collision shall not be ordered to give security for costs.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Examination for discovery of plaintiff

**[[500]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-500 .sectionLabel}** Notwithstanding
subsection 236(2), in an action in respect of a collision between ships,
a defendant may examine the plaintiff for discovery only after filing a
statement of defence and preliminary act.

## [PART 14]{.HLabel1}[Transitional, Repeal and Coming into Force]{.HTitleText1} {#h-1016548 .Part}

### [Transitional]{.HTitleText2} {#h-1016549 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Ongoing proceedings

- **[[501]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-501
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (2), these
  Rules apply to all proceedings, including further steps taken in
  proceedings that were commenced before the coming into force of these
  Rules.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Order for exceptions

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The Chief Justice of the Federal Court of Appeal or
  the Federal Court, as the case may be, may, by order, direct that rule
  380 shall not apply to certain proceedings or classes of proceedings
  before their court that are pending on the coming into force of these
  Rules until a date or dates set out in the order.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2004-283, s. 24
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Officers of the Court continued

- **[[502]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-502
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Every officer of the Court appointed
  under the Federal Court Rules prior to the coming into force of these
  Rules shall continue to act as if appointed under these Rules, until
  the appointment is revoked or another person is appointed in that
  officer's place.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Taxing officers continued as assessment
  officers

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of subsection (1), a reference in
  these Rules to an assessment officer shall be construed as a reference
  to a taxing officer appointed under the Federal Court Rules prior to
  the coming into force of these Rules.

**[[503]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-503 .sectionLabel}** [\[Repealed,
SOR/2004-283, s. 25\]]{.Repealed}

### [Coming Into Force]{.HTitleText2} {#h-1016563 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Coming into force

**[[504]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-504 .sectionLabel}** These Rules come into
force on April 25, 1998.

returned and readmitted immediately thereafter to the correctional
institution or other facility from which the said *(name)* was brought.

## [FORM 46]{.scheduleLabel}[Rule 46]{.OriginatingRef}[Warrant for Arrest (Defaulting Witness)]{.scheduleTitleText}  [(General Heading --- Use Form 66)]{.scheduleTitleText}  [Warrant for Arrest]{.scheduleTitleText}  {#form-46rule-46warrant-for-arrest-defaulting-witness-general-heading-use-form-66-warrant-for-arrest .scheduleLabel}
:::

TO ALL POLICE OFFICERS

AND TO OFFICERS OF ALL CORRECTIONAL INSTITUTIONS IN CANADA:

WHEREAS the witness *(name)*, of *(address)*, was served with a
[subpoena]{.otherLang lang="la"} to give evidence at the hearing of this
proceeding, and the proper attendance money was paid or tendered,

AND WHEREAS the witness failed to obey the summons, and I am satisfied
that the evidence of the witness is material to this proceeding.

YOU ARE ORDERED TO ARREST and bring the witness *(name)* before this
Court to give evidence in this proceeding, and if the Court is not then
sitting or if the witness cannot be brought before the Court, to deliver
him or her to a correctional institution or other secure facility to be
admitted and detained there until the witness can be brought before the
Court.

::: FlushLeft
![blank line](/img/ii_spacer.gif){.LeaderRightJustified-solid}
:::

::: FlushLeft
*(Signature of Judge)*
:::

![blank line](/img/ii_spacer.gif){.LeaderRightJustified-solid}

## [FORM 91]{.scheduleLabel}[Rule 91]{.OriginatingRef}[Direction To Attend]{.scheduleTitleText}  [(General Heading --- Use Form 66)]{.scheduleTitleText}  [Direction To Attend]{.scheduleTitleText}  {#form-91rule-91direction-to-attend-general-heading-use-form-66-direction-to-attend .scheduleLabel}
:::

TO: *(name of person to be examined)*

YOU ARE REQUIRED TO ATTEND AN EXAMINATION *(for discovery, in aid of
execution, etc.)* on behalf of *(identify party)* on *(day)*, *(date)*
at *(time)* at the office of *(name, address and telephone number of
examiner)*.

YOU ARE ALSO REQUIRED TO BRING WITH YOU and produce at the examination
the following documents and things: *(Set out the nature of each
document and thing and give particulars sufficient to identify each
one.)*

TRAVEL EXPENSES for *(number of days)* day(s) of attendance is served
with this direction, calculated in accordance with Tariff A of the
[Federal Courts Rules](/eng/regulations/SOR-98-106), as follows:

Transportation allowance ![blank line](/img/ii_spacer.gif){.Leader} \$
![blank line](/img/ii_spacer.gif){.Leader-solid}

Overnight accommodations and meal allowance ![blank
line](/img/ii_spacer.gif){.Leader}\$ ![blank
line](/img/ii_spacer.gif){.Leader-solid}

TOTAL![blank line](/img/ii_spacer.gif){.Leader}\$ ![blank
line](/img/ii_spacer.gif){.Leader-solid}

If further attendance is required, you will be entitled to additional
money.

THE EXAMINATION WILL BE CONDUCTED IN *(IDENTIFY OFFICIAL LANGUAGE)*. If
you prefer to be examined in the other official language, an interpreter
may be required and you must immediately advise the solicitor for the
party conducting the examination.

IF YOU FAIL TO ATTEND OR REMAIN UNTIL THE END OF THIS EXAMINATION, YOU
MAY BE COMPELLED TO ATTEND AT YOUR OWN EXPENSE AND YOU MAY BE FOUND IN
CONTEMPT OF COURT.

INQUIRIES CONCERNING THIS DIRECTION may be directed to *(name and
telephone number of contact person)*.

*(Date)*

::: FlushLeft
![blank line](/img/ii_spacer.gif){.LeaderRightJustified-solid}
:::

::: FlushLeft
*(Signature of solicitor or party)*
:::

::: FlushLeft
*(Name, address, telephone and fax number of solicitor or examining
party)*
:::

![blank line](/img/ii_spacer.gif){.LeaderRightJustified-solid}

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2004-283, s 35
:::
