## [FORM 458B]{.scheduleLabel}[Rule 458]{.OriginatingRef}[Interim Charging Order --- Securities]{.scheduleTitleText}  [(General Heading --- Use Form 66)]{.scheduleTitleText}  {#form-458brule-458interim-charging-order-securities-general-heading-use-form-66 .scheduleLabel}
:::

### [Interim Charging Order --- Securities]{.HTitleText1} {#interim-charging-order-securities .SchedHeadL1}

WHEREAS by a judgment *(or order)* made on *(date)* the defendant *(or
as the case may be)* was ordered to pay to the plaintiff *(or as the
case may be)* the sum of \$ *(amount)* and \$ *(amount)* in costs;

WHEREAS the sum of \$ *(amount)* remains due and unpaid;

AND WHEREAS the defendant *(or as the case may be)* has an interest or
right in the securities more particularly described in the schedule to
this order;

IT IS ORDERED that unless sufficient reasons to the contrary are shown
before *(date)*, at *(time)* when this matter will be further considered
by the Court at *(place)*, the defendant's *(or as the case may be)*
interest or right in the securities, including any beneficial interest,
to which the defendant *(or as the case may be)* is directly or
indirectly entitled, shall --- and it is ordered that in the meantime it
does --- stand charged with the payment of \$ *(amount)* including any
interest due on the judgment *(or order)* together with the costs of
this motion.

::: FlushLeft
![blank line](/img/ii_spacer.gif){.LeaderRightJustified-solid}
:::

::: FlushLeft
*(Signature of judge or prothonotary)*
:::

### [Schedule]{.HTitleText1} {#schedule-6 .SchedHeadL1}

*(Describe, with full particulars, the relevant shares, bonds or other
securities, stating their full title, their value and the name in which
they stand and whether the right or beneficial interest charged is in
the securities only or in the dividends or interest as well.)*

![blank line](/img/ii_spacer.gif){.LeaderRightJustified-solid}

## [FORM 459]{.scheduleLabel}[Rule 459]{.OriginatingRef}[Charging Order Absolute]{.scheduleTitleText}  [(General Heading --- Use Form 66)]{.scheduleTitleText}  [Order]{.scheduleTitleText}  {#form-459rule-459charging-order-absolute-general-heading-use-form-66-order .scheduleLabel}
:::

IT IS ORDERED that the interest of the defendant *(or as the case may
be) (name)* in the asset specified in the Schedule to this order stands
charged with the payment of \$*(amount)*, the amount due from the
defendant *(or as the case may be)* to the plaintiff *(or as the case
may be)* pursuant to a judgment *(or order)* of this Court dated
*(date)*, together with any interest due on the judgment *(or order)*
together with the costs of this motion *(in the amount of \$(amount), or
to be assessed*) which costs are to be added to the judgment debt.

::: FlushLeft
![blank line](/img/ii_spacer.gif){.LeaderRightJustified-solid}

## [FORM 486A]{.scheduleLabel}[Rule 486]{.OriginatingRef}[Bail Bond]{.scheduleTitleText}  [(General Heading --- Use Form 477)]{.scheduleTitleText}  [Bail Bond]{.scheduleTitleText}  {#form-486arule-486bail-bond-general-heading-use-form-477-bail-bond .scheduleLabel}
:::

I, *(full name and occupation of deponent)*, of the *(City, Town, etc.)*
of *(name)* in the *(County, Regional Municipality, etc.)* of *(name)*,
SWEAR *(or AFFIRM)* THAT:

- [1]{.lawlabel} I submit myself to the jurisdiction of this Court and
  consent that if *(insert name of party for whom bail is to be given,
  and state whether plaintiff or defendant, or as the case may be)*
  do*(es)* not pay what may be adjudged against them *(or as the case
  may be)* in this action, with costs, or do*(es)* not pay any sum due
  to be paid under any agreement by which the action is settled before
  judgment and which is filed in this Court, execution may issue against
  me, my executors or administrators, or my personal property or
  movables, for the amount unpaid or an amount of \$*(amount)*,
  whichever is the lesser.

*(Add where bond given by an individual:)*

- [2]{.lawlabel} I have a net worth of more than the sum of \$*(state
  amount in which bail is to be given)* after payment of all my debts,
  as shown by the financial statement attached as Appendix A hereto.

+------------------------------------------+------------------------------------------+
| ::: FlushLeft                                                                       |
| Sworn *(or Affirmed)* before me at the *(City, Town, etc)* of *(name)* in the       |
| *(County, Regional Municipality, etc.)* of *(name)* on *(date)*.                    |
| :::                                                                                 |

| *(or as the case may be)*                |                                          |
| :::                                      |                                          |

## [FORM 486B]{.scheduleLabel}[Rule 486]{.OriginatingRef}[Notice of Bail]{.scheduleTitleText}  [(General Heading --- Use Form 477)]{.scheduleTitleText}  [Notice of Bail]{.scheduleTitleText}  {#form-486brule-486notice-of-bail-general-heading-use-form-477-notice-of-bail .scheduleLabel}
:::

TAKE NOTICE that bail has been offered in the sum of \$*(amount)* on
behalf of the *(insert name of party on whose behalf bail is to be
given, and state whether plaintiff or defendant, or as the case may be)*
to answer judgment in this action by *(name of surety)*, as shown on the
guaranty or bail bond attached hereto.

AND TAKE NOTICE that unless a notice of objection to bail in Form 486C
of the [Federal Courts Rules](/eng/regulations/SOR-98-106) is served and
filed within 24 hours, bail will be given as provided in the attached
document, and a request will be made to release the arrested property in
accordance with rule 487 of those Rules.

(*Date*)

::: FlushLeft
![blank line](/img/ii_spacer.gif){.LeaderRightJustified-solid}
:::

::: FlushLeft
*(Signature of solicitor or party)*
:::

::: FlushLeft
*(Name, address, telephone and fax number of solicitor or party)*
:::

TO: *(Name, address, telephone and fax number of solicitor or party on
whom notice is served)*

![blank line](/img/ii_spacer.gif){.LeaderRightJustified-solid}

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2004-283, s. 28
:::
::::::::
:::::::::

## [FORM 486C]{.scheduleLabel}[Rule 486]{.OriginatingRef}[Notice of Objection To Bail]{.scheduleTitleText}  [(General Heading --- Use Form 477)]{.scheduleTitleText}  [Notice of Objection To Bail]{.scheduleTitleText}  {#form-486crule-486notice-of-objection-to-bail-general-heading-use-form-477-notice-of-objection-to-bail .scheduleLabel}
:::

TAKE NOTICE that the plaintiff *(or as the case may be) (name)* objects
to the bail given by *(name of surety)* on behalf of the defendant *(or
as the case may be) (name)* in this action.

The grounds for this objection are: *(Set out grounds.)*

(*Date*)

::: FlushLeft

:::

:::

TO: *(Name, address, telephone and fax number of solicitor or party on
whom notice is served)*

## [FORM 487]{.scheduleLabel}[Rule 487]{.OriginatingRef}[Release]{.scheduleTitleText}  [(General Heading --- Use Form 477)]{.scheduleTitleText}  [Release]{.scheduleTitleText}  {#form-487rule-487release-general-heading-use-form-477-release .scheduleLabel}
:::

TO the Sheriff of the *(County, Regional Municipality, etc. of (name),
or as the case may be)*:

WHEREAS by warrant issued *(date)* you were directed to arrest the ship
*(name) (or her cargo, etc. or as the case may be)* and to keep the same
under arrest until further order of this Court,

YOU ARE NOW DIRECTED to release the said ship *(name), (or her cargo, or
as the case may be)* from the arrest effected by virtue of that warrant.

*(Date)*

Issued by: [(*Designated Officer*)]{.FormBlank}

Address of local office: ![blank
line](/img/ii_spacer.gif){.Leader-solid}

This release was issued at the request of, and inquiries may be directed
to:

::: FlushLeft
*(Name, address, telephone and fax number of solicitor or party)*
:::

![blank line](/img/ii_spacer.gif){.LeaderRightJustified-solid}

## [FORM 490]{.scheduleLabel}[Rule 490]{.OriginatingRef}[Commission of Appraisal or Sale]{.scheduleTitleText}  [(General Heading --- Use Form 477)]{.scheduleTitleText}  {#form-490rule-490commission-of-appraisal-or-sale-general-heading-use-form-477 .scheduleLabel}
:::

*(Court seal)*

### [Commission of Appraisal]{.HTitleText1} {#commission-of-appraisal .SchedHeadL1}

(*or SALE, or APPRAISAL AND SALE, as the case may be*)

TO the Sheriff of the *(County, Regional Municipality, etc. of (name),
or as the case may be)*:

WHEREAS this Court has ordered that the ship *(name)*, *(or her cargo or
as the case may be)* be appraised *(and/or sold)*,

*(where appraisal only:)*

YOU ARE DIRECTED

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} to make a written inventory of the *(ship or cargo,
  etc., as the case may be)* and to choose one or more qualified persons
  and to swear that person or persons to appraise the *(ship or cargo,
  etc., as the case may be)* according to its true value; and

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} upon a certificate of that value having been reduced
  into writing, and signed by you and by the appraiser*(s)*, to file the
  certificate in the Registry of this Court together with this
  commission.

*(where sale only:)*

YOU ARE DIRECTED to cause the ship *(name)*, *(or her cargo or as the
case may be)* to be sold at public auction for the highest price that
can be obtained for it.

*(where appraisal and sale:)*

YOU ARE DIRECTED

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} to make a written inventory of the *(ship or cargo,
  etc., as the case may be)* and to choose one or more qualified persons
  and to swear that person or persons to appraise the said ship
  *(name)*, *(or her cargo or as the case may be)* according to the true
  value thereof; and

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} upon a certificate of such value having been reduced
  into writing, and signed by yourself and by the appraiser*(s)*, to
  cause the ship *(her cargo or as the case may be)* to be sold by
  *(public auction or private sale)* for the highest price that can be
  obtained for it, but no less than the appraised value unless the Court
  orders otherwise.

YOU ARE FURTHER DIRECTED, as soon as the sale has been completed, to pay
the proceeds thereof into Court and to file the certificate of
appraisement and an account of the sale signed by you, together with
this commission.

*(Add any other conditions ordered by the Court.)*

*(Date)*

Issued by: [(*Designated Officer*)]{.FormBlank}

Address of local office: ![blank
line](/img/ii_spacer.gif){.Leader-solid}

This commission was issued at the request of, and inquiries may be
directed to:

::: FlushLeft
*(Name, address, telephone and fax number of solicitor or party)*
:::

![blank line](/img/ii_spacer.gif){.LeaderRightJustified-solid}

the issue of any warrant for the arrest of the ship *(name) (or
description of other property)* without notice first being given to me.

AND I UNDERTAKE, within three days after being required to do so, to
give bail in this or any other action or counterclaim against that ship
*(or other property)* in this Court in the sum of \$*(amount)*, or to
pay that sum into Court.

MY ADDRESS FOR SERVICE AND TELEPHONE NUMBER are: *(address and telephone
number)*

## [TARIFF A]{.scheduleLabel}[(Rules 19, 20, 42, 43, 71 and 89)]{.OriginatingRef} {#h-1017822 .scheduleLabel}
:::

### [Court Fees]{.HTitleText1} {#court-fees .SchedHeadL1}

#### [Registry Fees]{.HTitleText2} {#registry-fees .SchedHeadL2}

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Fees payable on issuance

  - **1** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A party shall pay the following fees for the
    issuance of

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a statement of claim

      - [(i)]{.lawlabel} under section 48 of the [Federal Courts
        Act](/eng/acts/F-7)![blank
        line](/img/ii_spacer.gif){.LeaderRightJustified-dot} \$2

      - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} in a simplified action or in an appeal that
        proceeds by way of action ![blank
        line](/img/ii_spacer.gif){.LeaderRightJustified-dot} \$50

      - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} in any other action ![blank
        line](/img/ii_spacer.gif){.LeaderRightJustified-dot} \$150

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a statement of defence and counterclaim adding a
      party

      - [(i)]{.lawlabel} in a simplified action ![blank
        line](/img/ii_spacer.gif){.LeaderRightJustified-dot} \$50

      - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} in any other action ![blank
        line](/img/ii_spacer.gif){.LeaderRightJustified-dot} \$150

    - [(c)]{.lawlabel} a third or subsequent party claim

      - [(i)]{.lawlabel} in a simplified action ![blank
        line](/img/ii_spacer.gif){.LeaderRightJustified-dot} \$50

      - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} in any other action ![blank
        line](/img/ii_spacer.gif){.LeaderRightJustified-dot} \$150

    - [(d)]{.lawlabel} a notice of application ![blank
      line](/img/ii_spacer.gif){.LeaderRightJustified-dot} \$50

    - [(e)]{.lawlabel} a notice of appeal, other than appeals of
      prothonotaries' and referees' orders ![blank
      line](/img/ii_spacer.gif){.LeaderRightJustified-dot} \$50

    - [(f)]{.lawlabel} a [subpoena]{.otherLang lang="la"}

      - [(i)]{.lawlabel} in a simplified action ![blank
        line](/img/ii_spacer.gif){.LeaderRightJustified-dot}\$15

      - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} in any other proceeding ![blank
        line](/img/ii_spacer.gif){.LeaderRightJustified-dot}\$30

    - [(g)]{.lawlabel} a writ of execution

      - [(i)]{.lawlabel} in respect of a judgment in a simplified action
        ![blank line](/img/ii_spacer.gif){.LeaderRightJustified-dot}
        \$15

      - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} in respect of a judgment in any other
        proceeding ![blank
        line](/img/ii_spacer.gif){.LeaderRightJustified-dot} \$30

    - [(h)]{.lawlabel} an *Anton Piller* order, per defendant ![blank
      line](/img/ii_spacer.gif){.LeaderRightJustified-dot} \$50

  - [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Fees payable on filing

    [(2)]{.lawlabel} A party shall pay the following fees for the filing
    of

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a notice of motion for an extension of time to
      commence a proceeding ![blank
      line](/img/ii_spacer.gif){.LeaderRightJustified-dot} \$20

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a notice of motion for leave to commence a
      proceeding ![blank
      line](/img/ii_spacer.gif){.LeaderRightJustified-dot} \$30

    - [(c)]{.lawlabel} a notice of motion for summary judgment

      - [(i)]{.lawlabel} in an appeal that proceeds by way of action
        ![blank line](/img/ii_spacer.gif){.LeaderRightJustified-dot}
        \$100

      - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} in any other action ![blank
        line](/img/ii_spacer.gif){.LeaderRightJustified-dot} \$300

    - [(c.1)]{.lawlabel} a notice of motion for a summary trial ![blank
      line](/img/ii_spacer.gif){.LeaderRightJustified-dot} \$50

    - [(d)]{.lawlabel} a requisition for a pre-trial conference

      - [(i)]{.lawlabel} in a simplified action or an appeal that
        proceeds by way of action ![blank
        line](/img/ii_spacer.gif){.LeaderRightJustified-dot} \$100

      - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} in any other action ![blank
        line](/img/ii_spacer.gif){.LeaderRightJustified-dot} \$300

    - [(e)]{.lawlabel} a requisition under rule 155 to fix the time and
      place for the hearing of a reference

      - [(i)]{.lawlabel} in a simplified action or in an appeal that
        proceeds by way of action ![blank
        line](/img/ii_spacer.gif){.LeaderRightJustified-dot} \$50

      - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} in any other action ![blank
        line](/img/ii_spacer.gif){.LeaderRightJustified-dot} \$150

    - [(f)]{.lawlabel} a requisition for a hearing date in an
      application or appeal in the Federal Court ![blank
      line](/img/ii_spacer.gif){.LeaderRightJustified-dot} \$50

    - [(g)]{.lawlabel} a caveat warrant, caveat release or caveat
      payment ![blank
      line](/img/ii_spacer.gif){.LeaderRightJustified-dot} \$20

    - [(h)]{.lawlabel} an order of a tribunal under rule 424 in the case
      of a party other than the Crown ![blank
      line](/img/ii_spacer.gif){.LeaderRightJustified-dot} \$20

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the first document, in each separate claim, after
      the Court has ordered, pursuant to paragraph 106(a), that claims
      against one or more parties be pursued separately (plaintiff only)
      ![blank line](/img/ii_spacer.gif){.LeaderRightJustified-dot} \$150

  - [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Fees payable for paper copies

    [(3)]{.lawlabel} A person requesting paper copies of documents from
    the Registry shall pay \$0.40 per page.

  - [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Fees payable for digital recording

    [(4)]{.lawlabel} A person requesting a digital recording of all or
    part of any day of a proceeding from the Registry shall pay \$15 per
    recording.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Fees payable for trial or hearing

  **2** Where a trial or hearing in the Federal Court lasts more than
  three days, each party who participated at the trial or hearing shall
  pay a fee determined by applying the formula

  ::: {#1017872 .Section}
  \[(A × B) + C\] / D

  where

  [A]{.dfn}
  :   is
      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in respect of the hearing of a reference
        ordered under rule 153, \$75, and

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in respect of any other trial or hearing,
        \$150;

  [B]{.dfn}
  :   is the number of days of trial or hearing in excess of three;

  [C]{.dfn}
  :   is the amount payable by the Administrator to a court reporter in
      respect of the portion of the trial or hearing conducted after the
      first three days; and

  [D]{.dfn}
  :   is the number of parties who participated at the trial or hearing.
  :::

#### [Witnesses]{.HTitleText2} {#witnesses .SchedHeadL2}

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Witness fees

  - **3** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (2), a witness is
    entitled to be paid by the party who arranged for or subpoenaed his
    or her attendance \$20 per day plus reasonable travel expenses, or
    the amount permitted in similar circumstances in the superior court
    of the province where the witness appears, whichever is the greater.

  - [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Expert witness

    [(2)]{.lawlabel} Where a witness, other than a party, is an expert
    witness, the daily rate referred to in subsection (1) shall be
    \$100.

  - [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Additional costs to witness

    [(3)]{.lawlabel} A party may pay a witness, in lieu of the amount to
    which the witness is entitled under subsection (1) or (2), a greater
    amount equal to the expense or any loss incurred by the witness in
    attending a proceeding.

  - [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Amount established by contract

    [(4)]{.lawlabel} In lieu of the amounts to which an expert witness
    is entitled under subsections (1) and (2), a party may pay the
    expert witness a greater amount established by contract for his or
    her services in preparing to give evidence and giving evidence.

#### [Court Officers]{.HTitleText2} {#court-officers .SchedHeadL2}

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Services of court officers

  **4** Subject to section 5, the amount payable for the services of a
  sheriff or of a person referred to in subsection 89(2) shall be the
  amount permitted for similar services by the tariff of the superior
  court of the province in which the services were rendered.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Sheriff's services where no tariff
  provided

  **5** Where the practice of the superior court of the province in
  which a writ was executed does not provide for sheriff's fees
  realizable on execution, the following fees and disbursements are
  payable to a sheriff on execution:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} on the amount recovered up to and including
    \$1,000, five per cent of that amount;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} on the amount recovered in excess of \$1,000 and up
    to and including \$4,000, two and one-half per cent of that amount;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} on the amount recovered in excess of \$4,000, one
    and one-half per cent of that amount; and

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} mileage in respect of seizure and sale and all
    reasonable and necessary disbursements incurred in the care and
    removal of property.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2002, c. 8, s. 182
- SOR/2002-417, s. 29(E)
- SOR/2004-283, ss. 29, 33
- SOR/2013-18, ss. 19, 20
- SOR/2015-21, s. 33
- [SOR/2021-151, s. 26](#1303125-1305379){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1303125-1305379.html"
  role="button"}
:::

## [TARIFF B]{.scheduleLabel}[(Rules 400 and 407)]{.OriginatingRef} {#h-1017902 .scheduleLabel}
:::

### [Counsel Fees and Disbursements Allowable on Assessment]{.HTitleText1} {#counsel-fees-and-disbursements-allowable-on-assessment .SchedHeadL1}

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Bill of costs

  - **1** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A party seeking an assessment of costs in
    accordance with this Tariff shall prepare and file a bill of costs.

  - [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Content of bill of costs

    [(2)]{.lawlabel} A bill of costs shall indicate the assessable
    service, the column and the number of units sought in accordance
    with the table to this Tariff and, where the service is based on a
    number of hours, shall indicate the number of hours claimed and be
    supported by evidence thereof.

  - [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Disbursements

    [(3)]{.lawlabel} A bill of costs shall include disbursements,
    including

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} payments to witnesses under Tariff A; and

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} any service, sales, use or consumption taxes paid
      or payable on counsel fees or disbursements allowed under this
      Tariff.

  - [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Evidence of disbursements

    [(4)]{.lawlabel} No disbursement, other than fees paid to the
    Registry, shall be assessed or allowed under this Tariff unless it
    is reasonable and it is established by affidavit or by the solicitor
    appearing on the assessment that the disbursement was made or is
    payable by the party.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Calculation

  - **2** [(1)]{.lawlabel} On an assessment, the assessment officer
    shall determine assessable costs by applying the formula

    ::: {#1017919 .Subsection}
    A × B + C

    where

    [A]{.dfn}
    :   is
        - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the number of units allocated to each
          assessable service, or

        - [(b)]{.lawlabel} where the service is based on a number of
          hours, the number of units allocated to that service
          multiplied by the number of hours;

    [B]{.dfn}
    :   is the unit value as established in section 3 and adjusted in
        accordance with section 4; and

    [C]{.dfn}
    :   is the amount of assessable disbursements.
    :::

  - [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Fractional amounts

    [(2)]{.lawlabel} On an assessment, an assessment officer shall not
    allocate to a service a number of units that includes a fraction.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Unit value

  **3** The unit value as at January 1, 1998 is \$100.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Adjustment of unit value

  - **4** [(1)]{.lawlabel} On April 1 in each year, the Chief Justices
    of the Court of Appeal and the Federal Court, in consultation with
    one another, shall adjust the unit value by multiplying it by the
    amount determined by the formula

    ::: {#1017933 .Subsection}
    A/B × 100

    where

    [A]{.dfn}
    :   is the Consumer Price Index for all items for Canada, as
        published by Statistics Canada under the authority of the
        [Statistics Act](/eng/acts/S-19), in respect of December of the
        preceding year; and

    [B]{.dfn}
    :   is the Consumer Price Index for all items for Canada, as
        published by Statistics Canada under the authority of the
        [Statistics Act](/eng/acts/S-19), in respect of December 1994.
    :::

  - [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Rounding of result

    [(2)]{.lawlabel} Where a calculation under subsection (1) results in
    an amount that is not evenly divisible by 10, the resulting amount
    shall be

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} where it is less than 100, rounded to the next
      higher amount that is evenly divisible by 10; and

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} where it is greater than 100, rounded to the next
      lower amount that is evenly divisible by 10.

  - [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Communication of adjusted unit value

    [(3)]{.lawlabel} The Chief Justices shall without delay communicate
    adjustments to the unit value made under subsection (1) to their
    respective courts and to their assessment officers.

:::::: section
::::: {#1017951 .Schedule}
::: {}
## [SCHEDULE]{.scheduleLabel}[(Rule 52.2)]{.OriginatingRef}[Code of Conduct for Expert Witnesses]{.scheduleTitleText}  {#h-1017952 .scheduleLabel}
:::

#### [General Duty to the Court]{.HTitleText2} {#general-duty-to-the-court .SchedHeadL2}

- **1** An expert witness named to provide a report for use as evidence,
  or to testify in a proceeding, has an overriding duty to assist the
  Court impartially on matters relevant to his or her area of expertise.

- **2** This duty overrides any duty to a party to the proceeding,
  including the person retaining the expert witness. An expert is to be
  independent and objective. An expert is not an advocate for a party.

#### [Experts' Reports]{.HTitleText2} {#experts-reports .SchedHeadL2}

- **3** An expert's report submitted as an affidavit or statement
  referred to in rule 52.2 of the [Federal Courts
  Rules](/eng/regulations/SOR-98-106) shall include

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a statement of the issues addressed in the report;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a description of the qualifications of the expert
    on the issues addressed in the report;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the expert's current [curriculum vitae]{.otherLang
    lang="la"} attached to the report as a schedule;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the facts and assumptions on which the opinions in
    the report are based; in that regard, a letter of instructions, if
    any, may be attached to the report as a schedule;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} a summary of the opinions expressed;

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a report that is provided in
    response to another expert's report, an indication of the points of
    agreement and of disagreement with the other expert's opinions;

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} the reasons for each opinion expressed;

  - [(h)]{.lawlabel} any literature or other materials specifically
    relied on in support of the opinions;

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} a summary of the methodology used, including any
    examinations, tests or other investigations on which the expert has
    relied, including details of the qualifications of the person who
    carried them out, and whether a representative of any other party
    was present;

  - [(j)]{.lawlabel} any caveats or qualifications necessary to render
    the report complete and accurate, including those relating to any
    insufficiency of data or research and an indication of any matters
    that fall outside the expert's field of expertise; and

  - [(k)]{.lawlabel} particulars of any aspect of the expert's
    relationship with a party to the proceeding or the subject matter of
    his or her proposed evidence that might affect his or her duty to
    the Court.

- **4** An expert witness must report without delay to persons in
  receipt of the report any material changes affecting the expert's
  qualifications or the opinions expressed or the data contained in the
  report.

#### [Expert Conferences]{.HTitleText2} {#expert-conferences .SchedHeadL2}

- **5** An expert witness who is ordered by the Court to confer with
  another expert witness

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} must exercise independent, impartial and objective
    judgment on the issues addressed; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} must endeavour to clarify with the other expert
    witness the points on which they agree and the points on which their
    views differ.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2010-176, s. 13

## RELATED PROVISIONS {#related-provisions .scheduleTitleText}

- --- SOR/2021-150, s. 14

  - [14]{.lawlabel} Paragraph 292(a) of the Federal Courts Rules, as it
    read immediately before the day on which these Rules come into
    force, continues to apply to all actions commenced before the day on
    which these Rules come into force.
